        SELECT [a].[Id], [a].[Nome], [a].[Preco], [p1].[Id] AS [Id0], [p1].[ProdutoId]
        FROM [ProdutosAdicionais] AS [p1]

    FROM [Produtos] AS [p0]
    LEFT JOIN (
        SELECT [a].[Id], [a].[Nome], [a].[Preco], [p1].[Id] AS [Id0], [p1].[ProdutoId]
        FROM [ProdutosAdicionais] AS [p1]
        INNER JOIN [Adicionais] AS [a] ON [p1].[AdicionalId] = [a].[Id]
    ) AS [t] ON [p0].[Id] = [t].[ProdutoId]

) AS [t0] ON [c].[Id] = [t0].[CategoriaId]

    SELECT 1
    FROM [Produtos] AS [p]

FROM [Categorias] AS [c]
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT [p0].[Id], [p0].[Nome], [p0].[Descricao], [p0].[Preco], [p0].[ImagemUrl], [t].[Id] AS [Id0], [t].[Nome] AS [Nome0], [t].[Preco] AS [Preco0], [t].[Id0] AS [Id00], [p0].[CategoriaId]
    FROM [Produtos] AS [p0]
    LEFT JOIN (
        SELECT [a].[Id], [a].[Nome], [a].[Preco], [p1].[Id] AS [Id0], [p1].[ProdutoId]
        FROM [ProdutosAdicionais] AS [p1]
        INNER JOIN [Adicionais] AS [a] ON [p1].[AdicionalId] = [a].[Id]
    ) AS [t] ON [p0].[Id] = [t].[ProdutoId]
    WHERE [p0].[Ativo] = CAST(1 AS bit)
) AS [t0] ON [c].[Id] = [t0].[CategoriaId]
WHERE EXISTS (
    SELECT 1
    FROM [Produtos] AS [p]
    WHERE [c].[Id] = [p].[CategoriaId] AND [p].[Ativo] = CAST(1 AS bit))
ORDER BY [c].[Id], [t0].[Id], [t0].[Id00]

SELECT [c].[Nome], [c].[Id], [t0].[Id], [t0].[Nome], [t0].[Descricao], [t0].[Preco], [t0].[ImagemUrl], [t0].[Id0], [t0].[Nome0], [t0].[Preco0], [t0].[Id00]
FROM [Categorias] AS [c]
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT [p0].[Id], [p0].[Nome], [p0].[Descricao], [p0].[Preco], [p0].[ImagemUrl], [t].[Id] AS [Id0], [t].[Nome] AS [Nome0], [t].[Preco] AS [Preco0], [t].[Id0] AS [Id00], [p0].[CategoriaId]
    FROM [Produtos] AS [p0]
    LEFT JOIN (
        SELECT [a].[Id], [a].[Nome], [a].[Preco], [p1].[Id] AS [Id0], [p1].[ProdutoId]
        FROM [ProdutosAdicionais] AS [p1]
        INNER JOIN [Adicionais] AS [a] ON [p1].[AdicionalId] = [a].[Id]
    ) AS [t] ON [p0].[Id] = [t].[ProdutoId]
    WHERE [p0].[Ativo] = CAST(1 AS bit)
) AS [t0] ON [c].[Id] = [t0].[CategoriaId]
WHERE EXISTS (
    SELECT 1
    FROM [Produtos] AS [p]
    WHERE [c].[Id] = [p].[CategoriaId] AND [p].[Ativo] = CAST(1 AS bit))
ORDER BY [c].[Id], [t0].[Id], [t0].[Id00]

FROM [Categorias] AS [c]
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT [p0].[Id], [p0].[Nome], [p0].[Descricao], [p0].[Preco], [p0].[ImagemUrl], [t].[Id] AS [Id0], [t].[Nome] AS [Nome0], [t].[Preco] AS [Preco0], [t].[Id0] AS [Id00], [p0].[CategoriaId]
    FROM [Produtos] AS [p0]
    LEFT JOIN (
        SELECT [a].[Id], [a].[Nome], [a].[Preco], [p1].[Id] AS [Id0], [p1].[ProdutoId]
        FROM [ProdutosAdicionais] AS [p1]
        INNER JOIN [Adicionais] AS [a] ON [p1].[AdicionalId] = [a].[Id]
    ) AS [t] ON [p0].[Id] = [t].[ProdutoId]
    WHERE [p0].[Ativo] = CAST(1 AS bit)
) AS [t0] ON [c].[Id] = [t0].[CategoriaId]
WHERE EXISTS (
    SELECT 1
    FROM [Produtos] AS [p]
    WHERE [c].[Id] = [p].[CategoriaId] AND [p].[Ativo] = CAST(1 AS bit))
ORDER BY [c].[Id], [t0].[Id], [t0].[Id00]

        SELECT [a].[Id], [a].[Nome], [a].[Preco], [p1].[Id] AS [Id0], [p1].[ProdutoId]
        FROM [ProdutosAdicionais] AS [p1]

) AS [t0] ON [c].[Id] = [t0].[CategoriaId]

SELECT [c].[Nome], [c].[Id], [t0].[Id], [t0].[Nome], [t0].[Descricao], [t0].[Preco], [t0].[ImagemUrl], [t0].[Id0], [t0].[Nome0], [t0].[Preco0], [t0].[Id00]
FROM [Categorias] AS [c]
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT [p0].[Id], [p0].[Nome], [p0].[Descricao], [p0].[Preco], [p0].[ImagemUrl], [t].[Id] AS [Id0], [t].[Nome] AS [Nome0], [t].[Preco] AS [Preco0], [t].[Id0] AS [Id00], [p0].[CategoriaId]
    FROM [Produtos] AS [p0]
    LEFT JOIN (
        SELECT [a].[Id], [a].[Nome], [a].[Preco], [p1].[Id] AS [Id0], [p1].[ProdutoId]
        FROM [ProdutosAdicionais] AS [p1]
        INNER JOIN [Adicionais] AS [a] ON [p1].[AdicionalId] = [a].[Id]
    ) AS [t] ON [p0].[Id] = [t].[ProdutoId]
    WHERE [p0].[Ativo] = CAST(1 AS bit)
) AS [t0] ON [c].[Id] = [t0].[CategoriaId]
WHERE EXISTS (
    SELECT 1
    FROM [Produtos] AS [p]
    WHERE [c].[Id] = [p].[CategoriaId] AND [p].[Ativo] = CAST(1 AS bit))
ORDER BY [c].[Id], [t0].[Id], [t0].[Id00]

SELECT [c].[Nome], [c].[Id], [t0].[Id], [t0].[Nome], [t0].[Descricao], [t0].[Preco], [t0].[ImagemUrl], [t0].[Id0], [t0].[Nome0], [t0].[Preco0], [t0].[Id00]
FROM [Categorias] AS [c]
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT [p0].[Id], [p0].[Nome], [p0].[Descricao], [p0].[Preco], [p0].[ImagemUrl], [t].[Id] AS [Id0], [t].[Nome] AS [Nome0], [t].[Preco] AS [Preco0], [t].[Id0] AS [Id00], [p0].[CategoriaId]
    FROM [Produtos] AS [p0]
    LEFT JOIN (
        SELECT [a].[Id], [a].[Nome], [a].[Preco], [p1].[Id] AS [Id0], [p1].[ProdutoId]
        FROM [ProdutosAdicionais] AS [p1]
        INNER JOIN [Adicionais] AS [a] ON [p1].[AdicionalId] = [a].[Id]
    ) AS [t] ON [p0].[Id] = [t].[ProdutoId]
    WHERE [p0].[Ativo] = CAST(1 AS bit)
) AS [t0] ON [c].[Id] = [t0].[CategoriaId]
WHERE EXISTS (
    SELECT 1
    FROM [Produtos] AS [p]
    WHERE [c].[Id] = [p].[CategoriaId] AND [p].[Ativo] = CAST(1 AS bit))
ORDER BY [c].[Id], [t0].[Id], [t0].[Id00]
